**Also (HO 482–487), the `/lobbying` members-style redesign + the trades batch-fix verification — a chrome restructure + an operational verification, no theme-% change.** Two arcs cleared in one sweep. **Numbering:** HO 484 was drafted as the trades doc sweep but never issued; its content is folded here, so a future session should not hunt for a missing `484` file. This sweep is **487**. **(1) HO 482/483 — trades batch fix, VERIFIED against prod.** The `ingestChamber` DB writes in `lib/trades-ingest.ts` (~200 sequential `db.execute` `INSERT OR IGNORE` round-trips) were collapsed into one `db.batch(statements, "write")` (`ded2cf8`, shipped 2026-07-19 17:28). **The load-bearing correction on the verification itself:** the fix-time claim on record — "warm trades wall dropped 5–11.5s → 320ms" — is **not observable in `cron_runs`**, and the 14-day pull that was supposed to confirm it **straddles two mid-window fixes**, so the pooled figures are a trap (the naive full-window read shows a 63% `/api/sync` timeout rate + a 52s "breach", both fix-straddle artifacts, not current state). Split at the fix boundaries the story is clean and *confirms the prior arc*: **(a)** the route-timeout picture validates HO 480/481 (07-18 14:32) — **PRE-fix 79% timeout (34/43), p50 elapsed 55.2s; POST-fix 14% (2/14), p50 41.3s** — the route now finalizes `success` under the 55s soft cap on ~6 of 7 ticks, matching the documented ~1-in-5 baseline, the residual cap-hits being the by-design graceful reap (resumable sync continues next tick), not a regression. **(b)** the trades step validates HO 483 in *direction* — `payload.timings.trades` **PRE-483 p50 5.5s (n=11) → POST-483 p50 3.1s, min 1.9s (n=9)** — but the metric is the **whole `ingestTrades` step** (FMP page fetches for up to 3 pages × 2 chambers + name matching + the batch write), FMP-network-dominated, so the isolated 320ms DB-write win is muted inside a ~2–3s network floor and cannot be read off cron_runs directly. **A logged number is a claim, not a fact:** the 320ms is a real fix-time micro-measurement of the DB portion, but the coarse step metric is the only thing prod exposes and it only shows a consistent-direction p50 drop. **Re-probe triggers (banked: 52s route breach, or trades p90 > ~13s):** neither is crossed by post-fix steady state — post-fix route p50 41.3s (occasional cap-hits are the soft-cap-by-design, not a runaway), trades p90 11.5s under the 13s line (one pre-483 run touched 13.4s). **00:00-UTC contention oddity (banked at 483):** faint and inconclusive — success-only elapsed puts the midnight slot slowest (p50 46.5s vs 34–41s elsewhere), but n=5, it spans the fix boundary, and it is **not** trades-driven (midnight trades p50 3.3s is mid-pack); don't act on it. Verification script committed as `scripts/diagnostic/cron-runs-sync-verify-487.ts` (read-only). **(2) HO 485 — LDA filing-expand cost + shape probe, verdict GO.** The `/lobbying` inline row-expand runs a live per-click single-filing read; the probe cold-timed the worst case (`SEARCH lda_activities USING INDEX sqlite_autoindex_lda_activities_1 (filing_uuid=?)`, a PK-prefix seek — the **inverse of the HO 439 per-bill random-fetch trap that measured 44.5s**) at **75ms cold** (31-activity omnibus filing), typical **29–85ms**. Committed as `scripts/diagnostic/lda-filing-expand-cost-485.ts` (`3ed533c`); the durable LDA shape numbers it produced live in oddities. Note the follow-on `0614a66` typecheck guard — the probe pushed at `3ed533c` with a broken `npm run typecheck` (a `noUncheckedIndexedAccess` gap in the `pct` helper), undetected until the next session (oddities). **(3) HO 486 — the `/lobbying` members-style redesign.** Two commits: `2bc1a26` (chrome — an issue rail + filings content pane, scope via `?issue=`) and `0765aaf` (expandable rows + per-activity panel, carrying the `.lob-content .mc-row` A-regression fix and the orphaned-`IssueDrill` removal). One new query, `getFilingActivities`. **The deliberate divergence from `/members`:** scoping an issue serves the **precomputed `drill.recent` sample**, not a complete per-issue browse, and the pager stays on the **unscoped corpus feed only** — because a live per-code filings feed is the **HO 437 abandoned query (>25s cold)**, the thing the scoped-view divergence exists to work around (backlog). Prod-verified live at `0765aaf` (rail `ISSUES · 79` VOL-sorted, `AGE / REGISTRANT → CLIENT / ISSUES / BILLS` header, row carets, pager clamped `Page 1 of 40`, crosswalk + firms leaderboard below); headless verification is BotID-blocked (backlog). **No theme-% change** — a restructure of an existing surface + operational verification, no new theme surface (matching the LDA-arc precedent where 437 / 439–440 / 442 / 444 all held). Docs: HO 487. **Also notes now run through HO 487.**

**Also (HO 488–490), the first CI wiring + the #418 hydration arc — infrastructure + a correctness fix, no theme-% change.** Three HOs, and the arc closes on itself: **488 built the gate, 489 found the cause, 490 used the gate to make the cause unrepeatable.** **(1) HO 488 — CI wiring (`3aa677c`).** The **first CI in the repo** — there was none after the HO 475 Vercel-cron cutover retired the three GitHub Actions market workflows (and with them the whole `.github/` tree). `.github/workflows/ci.yml`: a single `checks` job running **typecheck + build** on `pull_request` and `push:main`, Node 20, `concurrency` with `cancel-in-progress`. **Secretless** — probed first: `next build` succeeds with `TURSO_*` and `GEMINI_API_KEY` unset because every DB-backed route is dynamic (`ƒ`), so no `env:` block and no repo secrets are needed. **Lint deliberately omitted** — no ESLint dependency or config exists in the repo, and wiring one fresh is its own arc (backlog), so forcing `next lint` would have produced a wall of pre-existing errors and blocked CI from landing clean. Verified two ways: a deliberate type error tripped `tsc` (TS2322, exit 2), and the Actions log was confirmed to show **real step execution, not skipped steps** (a green check on a skip-everything run is not a pass). No branch protection wired, so a red CI blocks nothing mechanically — it's the standing signal, not a hard gate. **(2) HO 489 — hydration probe (`c7d75f1`).** Root-caused the intermittent React **#418** on `/bills`, `/changes`, `/` (surfaced by the 488 CI's own smoke run): **client feed rows** (`BillRow`/`StagePillStrip`, `V2FeedList`, the expand panels) render a `Date.now()`-relative age at render time, so the SSR instant and the hydration instant land in different minute/hour/day buckets and React discards the server HTML. **Two plausible hypotheses killed** (oddities): it is *not* the `LAST SYNC` / HO 183 cycling stamp (`CyclingTimestamp`+`zone-cycle.ts` are hydration-safe by construction), and `/dashboard-v2` is a **`permanentRedirect("/")`, not a static page** — the failure attributed to it is really the home page after the 308, so the "static-staleness" theory was moot. Committed `scripts/diagnostic/hydration-probe-489.ts` — the forced-straddle harness (advance only the client's `Date.now()` before hydration). **(3) HO 490 — hydration fix (`8114ffc`, 34 files, +282/−75).** `formatRelativeAge` / `formatRelativeAgeLong` / `daysSince` now take a **required `nowMs`**; each page computes one `nowMs = Date.now()` and prop-drills it to the rows — no context provider, no default. **Required-with-no-default was the whole point:** a default would compile every existing call site untouched and let the bug walk back in silently; required means `tsc` rejects any implicit-clock caller, and **CI runs `tsc` (488)**, so the bug *class* is closed rather than the instance. The HO's 6-client-component sketch undercounted — the sweep actually reached ~14 pages + ~8 server wrappers (tsc's missing-arg errors drove completeness). Acceptance test = the 489 probe under forced straddle: **0 hydration errors across 6 loads** where it previously reproduced #418 every time. Also carries a **load-bearing force-dynamic invariant** (oddities): ages are now frozen server-side, so a route ever prerendered or given `revalidate` would produce a *silently wrong* age with no warning. **No theme-% change** — CI infrastructure + a correctness fix, no user-facing surface. Docs: HO 491. **Also notes now run through HO 491.**

**Also (HO 492–493), the `/lobbying` height compaction + feed-size tune — chrome + one constant, no theme-% change.** The `/lobbying` page ran ~4,897px tall (@1280w); two HOs took it to ~1,369px, all layout — no data-layer, no queries, no row internals. **(1) HO 492 — height compaction (`7564423`, 5 files).** Three chrome changes: the 79-code issue **rail bounded** (`.lob-rail-scroll`, 520px scroll + 31px header, internal scroll) so it stops setting the pane height with ~900px of dead whitespace beside it; **crosswalk + firms side-by-side** (`.lob-secs` 2-col grid, single-column ≤900px) instead of two full-width panels each wasting half their width; **both sections bounded** (`.lob-sec-scroll`, 340px, crosswalk topic-expansion scrolls inside the bound rather than clipping). Page **4,897 → 1,777px unscoped / 1,369px scoped**. All rules scoped to `.lob-*` (element-owned) so the shared `.mc-pane`/`.mc-rail`/`.mc-content` and `/members`/`/bill/[id]` stay byte-identical (globals.css diff purely additive). **The regression the bound introduced, and its fix** (oddities): bounding a list with a selected state broke selection visibility — scoping an issue low in the 79-item list re-rendered with it selected but the rail scrolled to top; fixed with `scrollIntoView({ block: "nearest" })` in `IssueRailRow` (scrolls the rail's overflow container only, not the window — verified on a deep `?issue=MON` pick). Rail bound chosen by **measuring** (scoped content ~553px, unscoped feed was the taller floor), not the mockup's number. **(2) HO 493 — feed page size tuned to the rail floor (`2fcb2a8`).** After 492 the bounded rail is the page-height floor (~551px), so any feed at/under that gives the same page height and the oversized feed only added dead scroll. `PAGE_SIZE` **25 → 13** (file-local to `app/lobbying/page.tsx`; `/bills` uses `FEED_PAGE_SIZE`, amendments/nominations have their own — zero blast radius). Unscoped now **1,369px**, section headers start ~887px (was ~1,250). **13 not 14** — the measured largest fit was 14 (17px slack) but feed rows are variable height (multi-bill-chip filings wrap ~33px), so 13's ~50px slack absorbs one wrapped row where 14 absorbs none (oddities: measured slack is a property of that day's feed, not the constant). **`MAX_FEED_PAGES` left at 40 deliberately:** reachable depth drops 1,000 → 520 filings while page 40's OFFSET falls to ~507 from ~975, so the deep-OFFSET clamp gets *cheaper*; holding depth would mean ~77 pages, worse. **No theme-% change** — a chrome restructure + one tuning constant on an existing surface (matching the LDA-arc precedent). Docs: HO 494. **Process observation (not a change):** the SKILL `/lobbying` entry has now gone stale twice in three arcs (HO 491 refresh, stale again by 492/493), both times because a shipped layout change didn't carry its own doc update — the cost of the code/docs-split convention, which is otherwise correct. Flagged, not acted on; if it recurs a third time the convention needs revisiting. **Also notes now run through HO 494.**

**Also (HO 495–496), the `/bills` two-pane redesign — probe → build, PARTIAL verdict, no theme-% change.** Third and final application of the `.mc-*` two-pane pattern (after committees on `/members` and LDA issue codes on `/lobbying`), so the pattern now spans all three category surfaces: committees, issue codes, CBT topics. **(1) HO 495 — filtered topic-distribution cost probe (`0d21148`, read-only diagnostic).** The rail's per-topic counts rebase on the other active filters — the query shape that produced the HO 382 `/?topics=` 500. Verdict **PARTIAL**: the bounded dims (stage/chamber/ceremonial) rebase cheaply (stage-present combos ~35ms; worst bounded — chamber/ceremonial alone — ~150–600ms, no costlier than the query already live on `/`), but **`q` is excluded** — it gives *zero* scan-narrowing (the query GROUP BYs all 24 topics regardless, so broad "act" at 29k matches and narrow "quantum" at 74 cost the same full-corpus walk) and every distinct search string would be its own unbounded cache key. No new index needed — the two HO 382 partial covering indexes suffice, and the stage-absent `INDEXED BY` hint stays mandatory or the planner regresses to the MULTI-INDEX-OR misplanner. **(2) HO 496 — the build, two commits.** `getBillTopicRailCounts` (`e84f7c5`) is a **new sibling**, deliberately NOT an extension of `getTopicDistribution` (widening that helper's two dashboard callers would silently reshape the treemap — the HO 486 riding-regression shape); it rebases on the bounded tuple only, zero-pads to 24 topics, and is self-excluding (never applies the topic clause). Then the two-pane chrome (`d789c20`): the 24-chip topic row becomes a **multi-select** `TopicRailRow` rail (the one divergence from the two single-scope rails — `?topics=` OR semantics preserved), the stage legend + party key fold into a keystrip, bill rows + client single-open expansion are unchanged, NEWS mode is untouched, and the CSS is purely additive under `.bl-*` so `/members`/`/lobbying`/`/bill/[id]` stay byte-identical. Live-verified: OR grows the count (HLTH+TAX 3,873 > HLTH 2,778), a stage filter rebases the rail, `q` leaves it unchanged, the three other pages render byte-identical. **No theme-% change** — a browse-surface restructure on an existing pillar. Docs: HO 497. **Also notes now run through HO 497.**

**Also (HO 499–501), the electoral-orphan sweep + the `/news` route extraction — no theme-% change.** **HO 499 (`2f6636c`), the HO 333 dead-component sweep:** deleted the four electoral orphans the Races+Primaries→Electoral consolidation (HO 333) left unreferenced — `RacesMap`, `PrimariesMap`, `PrimaryDistrictModal`, `PrimaryScrubber` — with the build compiling every electoral route as the safety net (a dangling import fails the build). **HO 500 (probe, read-only), the `/news` extraction scoping:** the premise going in was a greenfield route build; it wasn't. `app/news/page.tsx` **already existed as a redirect stub pointing INTO the thing it was meant to replace** (`/bills?mode=news`), so the real work was **inverting an existing arrow**, not laying a new one. Probe verdict: `NewsView` moves cleanly and there are **zero shared-component signature changes** — the HO 486 riding-regression pattern did *not* fire (the only edited component, `NewsFilters`, is single-consumer, confirmed by grep). Recommended: drop the cross-carry (option a), invert the redirect carrying `?bill=`, keep `mode` as a legacy alias. **HO 501 (two commits), the build:** `05ee430` adds an optional `basePath` prop to `NewsFilters` (additive, default `/bills`, byte-identical behavior) as a separable revert boundary; `ffdf243` is the atomic extraction — `/news` renders the feed (was the stub), carries **only** its five news params (`source`/`topic`/`window`/`bill`/`signal` + `page`), the eight-BILLS-param cross-carry is **dropped**, the redirect is **inverted** (`/bills?mode=news` → exactly one 307 → `/news`; `/news` itself does 0 redirects — the two-way-redirect brick avoided, curl-verified both directions), `mode` kept as a one-`if` legacy alias, the ⚡ `MediaAttentionCell` chip repointed to `/news?bill=`, and `breadcrumb` + `pathToNavKey` given `/news` lines. **`/bills` no longer carries ~10 params across two modes — the backlog's standing "`?mode=` is doing a route's job" observation is discharged.** (STEP 0 note: the `fit-finish.spec.ts:614` `/news` test PASSES against the new routes — its body only asserts loads-clean, never the redirect — so it's a title-rename, tracked as a separate code commit, not folded into these docs.) Docs: HO 502. **Also notes now run through HO 502.**

**Also (HO 503–504), the Phase-2 CI arc — a loop closed by replacing its own criterion, no theme-% change.** The Playwright-in-CI loop, carried since Jun 30 (HO 379), closed **backwards**: sent to build a fixture, it came back arguing against one. **HO 503 (probe, read-only, no commit) — verdict: don't build the fixture.** It was sent to cost the seeded local libSQL fixture the backlog had preferred since HO 491 (libSQL is SQLite, so a fixture removes the secrets question rather than managing it). Two results worth keeping though the verdict mooted them: `file:` works through `getDb()` **with zero `lib/db.ts` change** (the local sqlite path ignores the injected `boundedFetch` + the undefined `authToken`, so HO 238's HTTP timeout/retry semantics are provably untouched), and `migrate.ts` runs clean against `file:` (52 tables, FTS5 populated). Bucket count across both specs, 73 tests: **A 41 · B 28 · C 2 · D 2 · E 0** — 95% nominally fixture-servable. **The verdict turned on none of that.** It turned on the **guard inversion** (oddities): the specs guard with `if (await x.count()) … test.skip`, so an under-seeded fixture doesn't fail — it *passes*, exercising nothing. Green that proves nothing is worse than the red-blindness the fixture existed to cure; and pre-merge protection buys minutes in a single-committer flow that already diff-gates + prod-verifies every change, against a hundreds-of-row artifact maintained forever versus a 132-statement schema. **HO 504 (build, two commits) — the loop closed the other way.** `fc42872` (spec hardening) + `482cd55` (workflow). `.github/workflows/e2e-prod.yml`: an unattended prod crawl, `deployment_status` (gated `state=success` + `environment=Production`) + daily `0 15 * * *` + `workflow_dispatch`, running `e2e/smoke.spec.ts --grep-invert "@nonci" --retries=2`, `timeout-minutes: 20`, report artifact on failure, global concurrency `cancel-in-progress`. Deliberately a **separate workflow file, not a job in `ci.yml`** — it never runs on the PR path, so it can't become a required check that fails to report under the `paths-ignore`/branch-protection landmine `ci.yml` documents. **Step 0 (verified live, not assumed):** Vercel emits GitHub `deployment_status(state=success, env=Production)` to this repo, so post-deploy coverage is real; the event's `target_url` is the protected per-deploy alias, so the crawl runs against the stable prod domain instead, and the ~2min of checkout + `npm ci` + browser install is what makes that safe against the alias-swap race (the workflow comment says so as a constraint on future edits). Spec hardening: `isKnownNoise` extracted to `e2e/console-noise.ts` (single-source, both specs import it), the race-drawer test retargeted to `/electoral` with its skip-guard **converted to a hard assertion** (the cartogram is static topojson, so zero cells = regression), the bill-row selector re-anchored on the `role=button` + `aria-expanded` toggle contract, and the markets-hover case tagged `@nonci`. **Trigger coverage stated honestly:** `deployment_status` PROVEN (run `30040913867`, job ran-not-skipped, green 2m39s), `workflow_dispatch` PROVEN (run `30041280979`, exercises the `if:` fall-through branch `schedule` shares), **`schedule` UNPROVEN until its first fire** (can't be forced — tracked in backlog). **Two hardenings resolved, one retired:** #3 (bill-presence selector) shipped — but the backlog's description of it was **wrong on both counts** (it claimed an `<a href>` selector broken by `<li role=button>` rows; the actual code was a `.v2f-row` class selector against a `<div role="button">`); #2 (console allowlist) shipped as the shared extraction; **#1 retired as moot, not done** — "freeze the marquee so the hover lands" was specified before BotID was understood, and prod withholds the tape from headless Chrome entirely, so freezing a non-rendering animation fixes nothing. **No theme-% change** — test infrastructure, no user-facing surface (the HO 379 / 382–386 harness precedent); Foundation holds at 96. **Also notes now run through HO 504.**

**Also (HO 507, `eaf79a4`), the `/bill/[id]` detail-page redesign — panel-family hero + sectioned layout, no theme-% change.** The dedicated bill page had been *outclassed by its own hover preview*: the feed expand panel (HO 317) rendered a colored `BillStageBar` where the page showed a plain `StageIndicator`, carried RELATED NEWS the page lacked **entirely** — even though the ⚡ media-attention chip links *here* — and surfaced the summary up top, while the page buried it **6th down** on the one surface whose whole job is "WTF is this bill." So it was rebuilt as **the panel's big sibling**: a bordered hero reusing the shared `BillStageBar` + the `.bxp-body` 1.35fr/1fr grid (summary prose + RELATED NEWS via `getNewsForBill(bill.id, 5)` — the one added read, the exact query `/api/bill/[id]/panel` already runs — on the left, the boxed `.bxp-metabox` on the right), then member-hub section shells below: **COMMITTEES | HEARINGS** side-by-side via `.bdp-pair` (single-column ≤900px, **lone survivor renders full-width**), **AMENDMENTS** with the agreed/failed tallies folded into the header + a **340px scroll bound** (so `119-sconres-7`'s magnet render stops setting page height), **LOBBYING** with filings/clients folded up, and a one-row footer with a no-JS `<details>` raw-JSON toggle. **Four condensation moves** on the HO 492 fold-into-header precedent: the standalone `LATEST ACTION` section collapsed into the metabox, the amendment + lobbying stat lines pulled up into their shell headers, the footer squeezed to a single row. **Containment held** (the HO 486/492/496 additive-scope pattern): zero edits to the shared `.bxp-*`/`.v2f-bar` rules, every deviation additive under a new `.bdp` wrapper — the feed expand panel verified **byte-identical**. **`TopicTags` deleted** — `/bill/[id]` was its last consumer, closing the HO 316 holdout, so the B6 chip family (topic 316 · ID 466 · stage 467 · party 468) is now genuinely complete rather than "complete except one page"; `TopicChips` carries the metabox topics. The point-lookup `getBillById` gained **three plain `bills` columns** (`sponsor_bioguide_id`, `cosponsor_count`, `stage_changed_at`) — *not* because "the type lied": they're **optional** on the shared `FeedBill` row shape, so tsc could never have flagged the omitted SELECT (the check it can't run — oddities HO 508). And a **display-only** sponsor strip (`sponsorDisplayName()`, page-only) drops the `[P-ST]` embedded in `sponsor_name` so the colored `PartyTag` isn't double-printed — the query keeps the embedded form, the `BillAmendments` precedent. **No theme-% change** — a chrome restructure on an existing surface (the HO 486 / 492–493 / 495–496 browse-redesign precedent, every one of which held its bar); with this landing, **all three category browse surfaces (`/members` 328, `/lobbying` 486, `/bills` 496) plus the detail page now share one design vocabulary — the arc that opened at HO 328 is complete.** **Numbering — the 504 → 507 pointer jump skips no blocks:** HO 505 was the doc sweep *for* 503–504 (its content *is* that block, plus the SKILL testing-section refresh at `2c453af`), and HO 506 was docs-only (`22fd265` — backlog + oddities for the CA-cert pre-flight; no shipped surface, so no roadmap block was owed) — the HO 487 precedent for the skipped 484. Docs: HO 508. **Also notes now run through HO 507.**

**Also (HO 509–510), the shared record box — two detail pages onto one tabbed shell, no theme-% change.** The member hub was the last detail surface still in the pre-328 idiom — four unboxed strips stacked above the first section (`MemberHeader` / `MemberStats` / `MemberAffiliations` / `MemberFundraisingLine`, all floating on `--bg-base`), the DW-NOMINATE ideology axis buried *inside* the Voting-record section three sections down, and six full-width sections stacking to ~3,400px with two of them empty states ("No disclosed trades", "No recent news") on a typical House member; `/bill/[id]` had the same stack four sections deep. Both converged on one new `components/RecordTabs.tsx`. **HO 509 (`8538660`) — the component + the member hub.** A `"use client"` island on the `ActivityTabs` (HO 132/249) contract: holds **only** tab state, takes pre-rendered `ReactNode`s as props so the server page keeps every fetch; all panels mount with `hidden` (unmounting would drop an open `BillRowList` row on every swap); `tabs.length === 0 → null`. The hero became one bordered card in three bands — identity + a five-figure inline stat run (band 1), `MemberIdeology` **promoted out of Voting record** to the page's spine (band 2), and the metabox **unrolled sideways** into a `data-cells` N-up that collapses to 2-up/1-up when a cell is absent (band 3). Six sections → one box: Bills · Votes · Committees · Amendments · Press · Trades, plus a conditional **Scorecard** tab (Senate Democrats only — genuinely inapplicable, not merely zero). **Two components deleted**, both grep-confirmed single-consumer: `MemberAffiliations` (it re-printed the badges `MemberHeader`'s meta line already carried) and `MemberStats` (absorbed into the band-1 run). **The 509 follow-up, three fixes before the push:** `.rec-body` went from a fixed `430px` to `min-height: 260px; max-height: 430px` — **HO 244's no-jump rationale doesn't transfer**, because 244 protected content *below* its panel while the record box is the last element before the footer, so a swap moves only the footer; a bounded shift beat pervasive dead space on the majority of tabs. Internal out-links (`/bills?sponsor=`, `/trades?member=`) had regressed to plain `<a>` and were put back on `next/link` (external keeps `target="_blank"`). And the **election readout was printing twice** — `MemberHeader`'s chip *and* band 3's cell — so it consolidated into band 3, which also **fixed a live bug**: band 3 checked only `nextElectionYear != null` and rendered a stale "Next election 2024" where `MemberHeader` had correctly branched to "Former member." `MemberHeader` lost its election-chip block, its `rating` prop, and its **local `ratingColor()`** — the one carrying a "keep in sync with `RatingChip`" warning, so that duplicate map is discharged; the page imports the shared `lib/race-colors` one. **HO 510 (`d22fe66`) — the second consumer.** `/bill/[id]`'s four sections became the same box: Committees · Hearings · Amendments · Lobbying in **fixed legislative-process order** (referral → hearings → floor amendments → outside money, the order the sections already sat in). One additive prop, **`initialKey`**, opens the heaviest tab without the strip ever reordering — so `119-s-4784` opens on Amendments (872) and a one-referral bill opens on Committees. **The hero was not touched:** HO 507's containment rule held, no `.bxp-*` line moved, feed expand panel byte-identical. **Retired:** the page-local `SectionShell`, `.bdp-pair` (+ its `> section` rule and ≤900px query), `.bdp-amend-scroll` and `.bdp-out` — ~40 lines of `.bdp` CSS, three weeks after HO 507 landed it; the HO 507 stat lines that had been folded into section headers moved down one level into `.rec-note` sticky strips. **The two empty-state policies are opposite and both correct** — the arc's most transferable decision: member tabs render at zero and dim (`TRADES (0)` is a fact about a person), bill tabs are **omitted** at zero, so a bill with none of the four gets **no box at all**, preserving HO 507's section-omission exactly (see oddities). **`key={entity}` on both call sites** — `key={bill.id}`, `key={member.bioguideId}` — added as insurance against a same-instance route reconcile stranding the lazy-`useState` tab index, not a demonstrated bug (see oddities). **No theme-% change** — a chrome restructure on existing surfaces (the HO 486 / 492–493 / 495–496 / 507 precedent); it **extends** the HO 328 vocabulary arc the 507 block called complete rather than contradicting it — the browse surfaces converged on `.mc-*`, and the two detail pages have now converged on a second shared shell. Docs: HO 511. **Also notes now run through HO 510.**

**Also (HO 512, `af3ab90`), `?member=` on `/news` + the Press-tab out-link — no theme-% change.** The record box (HO 509) gave Bills + Trades out-links but left Press with nowhere to go — `/news` (HO 501) carried `source`/`topic`/`window`/`bill`/`signal` only. Three files, one commit: `/news` gains a `?member=` mode, and the member-hub Press tab gains `View press coverage →`. **The mechanism correction is the load-bearing content.** The backlog's close-path asserted `getNewsFeed` "already joins `observation_entities`" — **it doesn't**: `getNewsFeed` drives `news_mentions m INNER JOIN bills b` and never touches the observation tables. A person-filter bolted on there would have returned only articles that both name the member *and* matched a bill — a strict subset of what the Press tab previews (most of a member's coverage has no bill match), so the out-link would have landed on **fewer** rows than the tab it came from. So member mode is a **branch to the observation layer** — `getMemberNews`, the exact query the Press tab already renders — not a filter on the mention feed, and it ships **zero `lib/queries.ts` changes** (the HO 501 route-split precedent: the honest fix was structural, not another param on the existing query; see oddities). **Precedence:** when `member` parses it owns the view — `bill`/`source`/`topic`/`window`/`signal`/`page` are ignored and not carried, and the pill's ✕ returns to bare `/news`; `getMember` null (valid-shape but unknown bioguide) renders member mode with the raw id + empty state (never a 404), and a parse-fail falls through to the unscoped feed. **The filter-bar asymmetry, stated as a rule so it isn't "fixed" later:** member mode early-branches `NewsFilters` to render *only* the member pill — observation rows carry no source/topic/window/signal dims, so dead chips would imply filtering that isn't happening; bill mode keeps its chips because bill-scoped rows are still `news_mentions`. Both correct. **The cap asymmetry, disclosed:** the tab previews `getMemberNews(…, 8)`, the out-link serves `NEWS_MEMBER_CAP = 50` (file-local, the HO 493 precedent), so the out-link deliberately lands on *more* rows than the tab — the opposite direction from Bills/Trades (whose out-links exist because their counts exceed the shown rows). Hence **no count in the label**: `count: news.length` is a cap, not a total, and "View all N" would be a lie. **Not done (recorded so it isn't re-litigated):** no `getObservationNews` fold — the HO 414 debt triggers on a 3rd *projection*, and this is a 3rd *caller* of the existing helper at a raised limit (the HO 470 "adds callers, not a third projection" line); no pagination, no total-count query, no `getNewsFeed` change of any kind; the `/news` source-spine two-pane stays a separate QUEUED follow-on (this added a mode, not the rail). **No theme-% change** — a cross-link on existing surfaces (the HO 509–510 precedent); it **discharges** the backlog's Press-out-link item in full. Docs: HO 513. **Also notes now run through HO 512.**

**Also (HO 514–515), the `/news` topic rail — the 4th and final `.mc-*` two-pane, no theme-% change.** The `.mc-*` two-pane pattern now covers **every browse surface**: committees (`/members` 328) → LDA issue codes (`/lobbying` 486) → bill topics (`/bills` 496) → **news topics (`/news` 515)** — the arc that opened at HO 328 is complete across browse surfaces (the detail pages converged separately on `RecordTabs`, HO 509–510). **The respec is the block's real content:** the banked item named **SOURCES** as the spine, but `NEWS_SOURCES` has **three** entries against 24 / 79 / ~200 on the shipped rails — three rows is the SOURCE chip row relocated, not a spine. So the rail shipped on the **TOPIC** axis instead, and the SOURCES idea is **dead on cardinality, not deferred**. **HO 514 (`f7a3de4`), the probe — GO, live-servable, hint mandatory:** unhinted bare **2231ms** cold (the MULTI-INDEX OR off `idx_bills_is_ceremonial` — the HO 332/479–481 drive-order class firing on a *fourth* surface), `signal=breaking` 943ms, vs **29–64ms hinted** across every combination; when `source` is set the planner picks `idx_news_mentions_source` itself but hinting there costs nothing (31–33ms), so **one always-hinted query** beat a conditional. No new index. Corpus **re-measured 227 → 726 rows / 439 distinct articles** (3.2×, not order-of-magnitude, so the live call stands — the stale 227 lived in 4 places incl. 2 code comments, corrected across HO 516's two commits). **HO 515, two commits:** `a2c825d` — `getNewsTopicRailCounts`, a **new sibling** of `getNewsFeed` (not an extension — the HO 496 `getBillTopicRailCounts` reasoning), self-excluding on `topic`, rebased on source/window/signal, `unstable_cache` tag `news-breaking` (no new cache wiring), `try/catch → null`, 24 topics zero-padded VOL-desc; `8bb9618` — the `.mc-pane nw-pane` chrome. **Two calls recorded as rules:** (a) **the rail counts DISTINCT ARTICLES, not mentions** — the feed's own total is distinct-articles (HO 133), so a mentions-counting rail would print a number the feed contradicts on click (the HO 442 structural-coherence discipline); enforced by extracting the dedup expression to one module-level **`NEWS_ARTICLE_KEY_SQL`** both consume, so they *can't* drift (the `getNewsFeed` refactor was pure substitution, feed verified unchanged; live: `?topic=government_operations` → rail 43 === feed 43). (b) **single-select rail**, a new `NewsTopicRailRow` on the `/members` + `/lobbying` `.mc-crow.is-sel` idiom — **not** a lift of `/bills`' `TopicRailRow`, whose multi-select checkbox is the documented *divergence* (plural `?topics=` OR) against `/news`'s singular `?topic=`; widening the `/bills` component with a mode flag was rejected as the HO 486/496 riding-regression shape. **Three modes, stated as contract:** default → rail renders; `?bill=` → no rail (one bill is a single-topic slice); `?member=` → no rail (different table — HO 512's mode comes out byte-identical, `hideTopics` left off so its chip row returns). The hidden rail in member mode is a **deliberate seam Axis B reopens**, not an oversight. **CSS additive under `.nw-*`** — diff-confirmed no shared `.mc-`/`.lob-`/`.bl-`/`.bxp-`/`.bdp-` rule edited, so `/members` / `/lobbying` / `/bills` are byte-identical (the containment rule has now held through 486 / 492 / 496 / 507 / 515). **Axis B, banked with its constraint (the forward-looking part):** an **"IN THE NEWS" member group as a SECOND group in this same rail**, off the observation layer — probe cardinality **126 bioguides ≥1 obs, 39 ≥3, 10 ≥10**, head genuinely newsy (Graham 49 · Thune 29 · Collins 21 · McConnell 20), **100% of resolved bioguides hit a live `members` row**, rollup 34ms off `idx_obs_entities_type_value`. **The load-bearing constraint: the member group needs its OWN window, decoupled from `?window=`** — at 24h the head collapses to 6 members (0 at ≥3), so rebasing it on the page window empties it; and the observation layer is only ~30d old (HO 394 pilot), so 30d ≈ all-time *today* and that equivalence will stop holding. **No theme-% change** — a browse-surface restructure on an existing pillar (the 486 / 492–493 / 495–496 / 507 precedent). Docs: HO 516. **Also notes now run through HO 515.**

**Also (HO 517, `a71a1d4` query · `f1b1167` chrome), the `/news` "IN THE NEWS" member rail group — the Axis B seam closed, no theme-% change.** The forward item the HO 514–515 block banked (a second rail group off the observation layer), built with **no probe step** — HO 514's cardinality/cost numbers stood up unmodified, the payoff of measuring both axes in one probe. The `?member=` seam HO 515 left deliberately open (member mode rendered no rail) is now closed: the `.nw-rail` carries **two groups** (TOPICS + IN THE NEWS) inside its one bounded `.nw-rail-scroll`, and member mode is a first-class rail selection rather than a deep-link-only view. **The query (`a71a1d4`):** `getNewsMemberRailCounts()` off `observation_entities` (`entity_type='person'`) → `observations`, `INNER JOIN members` for name+party, ranked desc + name-tiebroken, returns `{ rows, total }`; EXPLAIN-confirmed it drives `idx_obs_entities_type_value`, `unstable_cache` tag `member-news` (already flushed by the news cron + allowlisted on `/api/revalidate`, HO 414 — no new wiring), `try/catch → null` hiding **only this group** (TOPICS + feed still render). Plus `getNewsMemberRailPin(bioguide)`, an **uncached point-lookup** for the pin — a bounded `activeBioguide` arg on the counts query would make every visited member its own cache key, so the separate lookup keeps the counts cache clean. `NEWS_MEMBER_RAIL_WINDOW_DAYS = 30` is exported so the header label and the query can't drift. **Four design calls, all verified live:** (1) **fixed 30d window, disclosed** — the member group does NOT rebase on `source`/`window`/`signal` (header `IN THE NEWS · 30d`); 30d not "all-time" because the observation layer is ~30d old (HO 394 pilot) so they're equal today but the label stays correct as the corpus accrues (the HO 456 disclose-the-exclusion posture) — verified Graham holds 49 at `?window=24`. (2) **units match** — HO 394 writes one observation per fetched article, so both groups count **articles** (the HO 442 coherence that lets two count columns share one rail; recorded in a comment). (3) **one selection across both groups** — the two groups drive different panes (topic → mention feed, member → observation feed) so they can't co-activate; `NewsTopicRailRow.scope()` now also clears `member` and selecting a member clears `topic` (topic↔member mutually clear, exactly one lit row spanning both groups). (4) **top 15, total disclosed** (`… · 15 of 126`), with the active member **pinned** at the group top when outside the shown set — the one path the rail can't reach from itself (the HO 512 Press-tab out-link can send any of ~535 members here), so without it a low-coverage arrival renders a rail with nothing lit: the HO 492 selection-invisibility failure through a different door. **The zero-count pin holds:** a pinned member with 0 recent observations still renders, lit, count 0 (the pane below is showing their coverage, so the rail must agree they're the active scope). **Chrome (`f1b1167`):** a new single-select `NewsMemberRailRow` (party dot via `lib/race-colors`, not a local map — HO 468; surname-forward + count, no bar), both groups sharing the one `.nw-rail-scroll` (no nested scroll), `scrollIntoView({block:"nearest"})` covering the member group too; CSS **additive under `.nw-*`** (a `.nw-rail .mc-crow.nw-mrow` grid override + dot + pin hairline + group-header border) — diff-confirmed no shared `.mc-`/`.lob-`/`.bl-`/`.bxp-`/`.bdp-` rule touched, the **sixth** consecutive test of the containment rule (486 / 492 / 496 / 507 / 515 / 517). **Mode contract now:** default → both groups selectable; `?topic=` → topic lit, mention feed; **`?member=` → rail renders** (the 515 seam closed — TOPICS shows bare counts, IN THE NEWS shows the member lit/pinned), the HO 512 observation-feed pane below unchanged; `?bill=` → still no rail, byte-identical. Prod-verified across bare (15 rows, Graham head), `member=Lawler` (rank ~30, injected+lit, `nw-mrow-pin`×1, observation feed), `member=Aderholt` (0 recent obs, pinned+lit, count 0 — the call-4 zero case), the window-independence check, and `?bill=` unchanged; `tsc` clean, `verify:deploy` served `f1b1167` === HEAD. **No new nav, migration, cron, or index; the 35 unjoinable person rows (no bioguide) stay excluded (the HO 398 challenger edge); SOURCES stays dead on cardinality (HO 514).** **No theme-% change** — a browse-surface add on an existing pillar (the 486 / 492–493 / 495–496 / 507 / 514–515 precedent). With this, the `.mc-*` two-pane arc that opened at HO 328 is complete across every browse surface **and** the `/news` rail now carries the member axis the observation layer was built to enable. Docs: HO 518. **Also notes now run through HO 517.**

**Also (HO 519–520), the 2nd full re-derivation + the SC seat-change fix.** **HO 519 (read-only audit, §0–§5 against `5681322`):** the deferred "next full audit," done as re-derivation #2. **Structurally green** — sync fresh (`MAX(update_date)` today, `bills` 17,027 == `bills_fts`), tracked corpus **16,657** (`getCorpusStats(true)`, up from 15,075 at HO 404), summarize queue drained to 4; `/api/health` 200 with **all 14 crons fresh**; the **four fixed loops holding** in steady state — summarize (406) not re-grown, weekly-report (407) present + catch-up healthy, `/api/sync` trailing-step (480/481) at **77% → 7%** post-fix timeout (the residual ~55s reaping is the by-design HO 477 soft-cap, not failure), trades (483) step p90 **10,530 → 5,600ms** (clear of the ~13s re-probe line); cold-plan audit **0 unexpected BAD of 50**. **Cold-plan gap-closer:** `cold-start-audit-332`'s SQL inventory predates the LDA / amendments / nominations / news-rail pillars, so its "0 BAD" is blind to them — the audit **hand-EXPLAIN'd 9 post-404 queries** (the `*RailCounts` trio, the amendments disposition/aggregate scans, the nominations GROUP BYs, the `getBillLobbying` seek), all riding their intended index or covering index (extending the script is a **standing observation**, not owed here). **Two ratified bar decisions:** **News signal CLOSED → 98** — the "obs layer joinable" label under-sold a now-full pillar (`/news` route 501 + `?member=` 512 + topic rail 515 + member rail 517, race→news 398, member→news 414, dashboard NEW badge 470); `98` not higher because the residue is gated/inert (the observation envelope grading/cluster/lineage fields, the policy-hook→bill/topic linkage, the the_hill budget soak), not active gaps. **New combined bar `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` = 92** — one bar (the 435–463 "extend downstream along the bill spine" arc; the `/patterns`-class aggregate+drill shape; each individually thinner surface than Member/Races; naming matches the `Committee activity / hearings` slash style); `92` = v1-complete across all three (data + aggregate + detail + cross-links) with real banked gaps (LD-203 money surface, military-noms view + `/nomination/[id]`, amendment→roll-call `recordedVotes` linkage, the `/lobbying` fbar features + description clamp). **Overall ~98 → ~97**, recomputed from the reconciled **9** bars; **all other bars held** (Foundation 96 / Home 100 / Visualizations 97 / Weekly reports 98 / Member depth 99 / Races 99 / Committee activity / hearings 98). **HO 520 (SC seat-change fix — data-only, zero commits):** Sen. Lindsey Graham (`G000359`, R-SC, class 2) **died** (served 2003–2026); Gov. McMaster appointed **Darline Graham (`G000608`)** as caretaker until 2027-01-03 — **verified against governor.sc.gov** (the HO 422 Kiley verify-before-fix discipline: a logged "wrong-incumbent" was a real post-cutoff event, not bad yaml). CBT's stale roster (`G000359` still `is_current=1`, `G000608` absent) + the `S-SC-2026` incumbent were fixed by the routine **`sync:members` → `sync:crosswalk` → `backfill:races`** (the HO 402 Gallagher-staleness class recurred), the **HO 410 audit re-ran fully green** (§1/§2/§4 all 0/0), and the live `/electoral` + `/race/S-SC-2026` now render Darline Graham — **zero code, zero commits**. The **Aug-11 special-primary re-ingest is parked** (SC 2026 became an open special — special primary Aug 11, runoff Aug 25, general Nov 3; the field's still forming, so the stored June-9 regular primary re-ingests once results exist — the "re-poll as the field settles" pattern). Docs: HO 521. **Also notes now run through HO 520.**

**Also (HO 522–525), the member-scorecard participation arc — B1 chamber-context + B2 career rollup, Member depth 99 → 100.** **HO 522 (source probe):** participation **GO**; the "VoteSmart" idea was **NO-GO on *access* not join** — the icpsr↔votesmart join was already solved in `member_ids.votesmart`, but the SmartVote/VoteSmart API is retired behind a Cloudflare/ToS wall, so it's an access dead-end; **broader external scorecards reframed from a hold to optional breadth** (LCV CSV-export + Heritage bioguide-keyed URLs both viable-but-unbuilt, ADA dropped stale, USCPR already ships as one instance). **HO 523 (B1, `2b8d62b`):** the 119th missed-rate was **already on the hero** — the delta was chamber-median **context** beside it (`getChamberParticipationContext`, a cached all-member distribution, **no new table**); **floor F=50** over the population for both the median and rank-eligibility (floored only 3 departed members, clips zero current). Median shipped in the hero; a bare attendance **rank was computed-then-dropped** (delegate-inflated + unused — the drop-unused-field call). **HO 524 (cost probe):** streaming ingest **GO** — 52 files 94→119, ~356MB, flat ~130MB peak (the accumulator is 536 members × a few ints, so memory doesn't grow with range; stream per-file, discard, next). **HO 525 (B2, `bf63b23`):** `member_career_votes` rollup via manual `sync:career-votes` (truncate-then-recompute off Voteview's per-Congress votes files, current-only via an `is_current=1` join), the career stat on the hub beside the 119th (`Missed · career · N Congresses`); cross-checks passed (Collins 0/10,062 since 1997, Grassley 1.09%/26C, Fetterman 13.1%), Graham (no icpsr) → graceful null. **Two data findings worth the oddities ledger:** (1) Voteview `cast_code` **7/8 = Present = participation**, so the correct split is **cast = 1–8 / miss = 9 / not-seated = 0-or-absent** — the coarse {0,1–6,9} model the HO 524 handoff carried was **wrong**, caught only by parsing real rows; (2) **non-voting delegates carry structurally-inflated miss-rates** (can't vote on final passage), so a *rank* surface needs a delegate-eligibility carve-out while a member's *own-rate* display keeps it (B1/B2 precedent). **Member depth 99 → 100** — the substantive participation gap shipped end-to-end, the "external scorecards" half reframed optional (see provenance). No new nav / cron / migration beyond the one rollup table; the probe + cost scripts stay untracked. Docs: HO 526. **Also notes now run through HO 525.**

**Also (HO 527), the `/members` participation dotplot — the participation arc's population cut, no bar move.** The population twin of the member-hub participation display (B1 523 + B2 525), built as `IdeologyStrip`'s sibling — the exact member-hub→`/members`-dotplot progression the ideology arc took (hub axis 421 → dotplot 425). Shipped + prod-verified; live SHA `7fa729c`. **`getParticipationStrip()`** (`lib/queries.ts`) is a new sibling of `getIdeologyStrip` (NOT an extension of `getChamberParticipationContext`, which returns only per-chamber medians): every floored current member (`is_current=1`, `HAVING total >= PARTICIPATION_FLOOR`, metric `not_voting/total` — the **identical B1 population**, so the strip's client-computed chamber medians echo the hub context by construction) plus an `is_delegate` flag; small-table population read, no `INDEXED BY`/no new index (the `getIdeologyStrip`/band regime), `unstable_cache` tag `votes`/3600. **`ParticipationStrip`** (`components/ParticipationStrip.tsx`) is `IdeologyStrip` cloned with the metric deltas: domain **0..CAP=30** (locked from STEP 0's non-delegate max 29.02, not a mockup number), 0.5pp Wilkinson bins, dots **party-colored** (so high-miss clustering by party reads — roadmap lens #4), median ticks on the **CHAMBER split** (HSE/SEN, neutral amber — NOT party, the meaningful split since the House votes far more than the Senate), no empty-center annotation (participation is unimodal-skewed). Placed **open directly below** `IdeologyStrip`, rescoped by the same chamber toggle only (`partDots` mirrors `stripDots`); CSS **additive under `.part-strip-*`**, `IdeologyStrip` byte-identical (the containment rule's next test). **The delegate carve-out is the load-bearing detail (the HO 523/525 banked constraint, now discharged for the dotplot):** non-voting delegates are excluded from both cloud and median via the territorial predicate `chamber='house' AND state IN ('DC','AS','GU','MP','PR','VI')` (there is no delegate flag — the 6-code set catches exactly the 6 non-voting members), with the excluded count disclosed in the footer (the LDA/nominations disclose-the-exclusion posture). **The STEP-0 finding corrected a premise (oddities):** the carve-out is **population-correctness, not outlier-trimming** — only 2 of 6 delegates (Radewagen 82%, Plaskett 50%) are extreme outliers; the real high-miss tail is a **voting** member (Frederica Wilson 29% > 4 of the 6 delegates), which is what sets CAP. So delegates are dropped because they're structurally ineligible on final passage, NOT because they're the high scores. Prod-verified: HSE med 1.9% / SEN med 2.1% (= the B1 medians), chamber toggle rescopes (ALL 2 ticks 530/6 · HOUSE 1 tick 431/6 · SENATE 1 tick 99/0), delegates absent from the cloud, hover→hub. **Member depth holds at 100** — an enabling surface, no bar move (the HO 425 dotplot precedent, which also held). **Banked from the arc, still unbuilt:** a participation *sort/rank* on the roster (the carve-out constraint still binds it) + a career-chamber-median context (cheap from `member_career_votes`, deliberately unbuilt to avoid a 4-stat hero). Docs: HO 528. **Also notes now run through HO 527.**

**Also (HO 529–530), amendment roll-call votes on the bill hub — the Senate half of the banked `recordedVotes` gap, query-only, no theme-% change.** The HO 452 `recordedVotes` lead (the capability that block banked when the amendments status model closed NO-GO), built probe-first. **HO 529 (`e0131cf`, read-only probe) retired the backlog's premise.** The banked item assumed a member-level "how did each member vote on this amendment" surface needs an **`/actions` walk** (~2× the amendments backfill) to recover each voted amendment's roll-call number. The probe found that **half wrong and the handoff's own candidate join dead**, payoff intact via two different keys per chamber (the "a backlog line's stated mechanism is a claim — probe it" rule, HO 512/514 class): **(1) `votes.amendment_designation` is dead for amendments** — it only holds nomination/treaty designations (the syncs tag amendment votes to the underlying bill or to nothing), 0/431 join. **(2) Senate is FREE (GO-CHEAP):** Senate amendment votes carry the number in `votes.question` (`On the Amendment S.Amdt. 14 to …`), a regex parse joins **114 SAMDT, 100% on tracked bills, `member_votes` present** (99–100/vote) — query-only, zero API, zero backfill, retiring the walk premise for the Senate. **(3) House needs the walk (GO-WALK, linkage-only):** House vote questions are generic (`On Agreeing to the Amendment`, no number, only `bill_id` — can't disambiguate which amendment), so a text parse can't do the House; the `/actions.recordedVotes.rollNumber` walk resolves **100% to existing `votes` rows** (BLOCKED-ON-SYNC refuted — the roll calls are all ingested), so the House is a bounded *linkage* backfill (store amendment↔`vote.id`), NOT a member-vote backfill. **(4) The buildable surface is small but clean** — ~114 SAMDT free + the rare HAMDTs recoverable via the walk (the HO 461 "the floor almost never votes" finding reinforced: 17/20 sampled HAMDTs never got a recorded vote), all on tracked bills, full member positions. **HO 530 (`1f9f748`) ships the Senate half — query-only.** `getBillAmendmentVotes(billId)` is a **separate** cached query (the HO 496/512 new-sibling discipline — `getBillAmendments` byte-identical; the regex parse can't live in its SQL): pulls the bill's SAMDT amendments, parses matching Senate votes from `votes.question`, attaches the tally (on the `votes` row directly — `yea_count`/`nay_count`/`result`) + the **D/R/I party split** from `member_votes` (a bounded per-vote aggregate), returns `Map<amendmentId, AmendmentVote[]>` (a **list** — 3 amendments corpus-wide carry a table+substantive pair). Rendered as a compact vote line under acted amendments in the `/bill/[id]` AMENDMENTS section — `Rejected 45–55 · D 41-4 · R 2-51 · I 2-0` (party-colored via the shared `partyColor`, HO 468) — and the display-only disposition dot **becomes authoritative from `votes.result` where a vote exists** (via the module `deriveDisposition`, no drift), keeping the keyword scan only for the no-vote majority. Byte-identical where no vote (~99% of rows — the absence is the signal, the HO 527 omit-don't-disclose-per-row call). **No cost probe** — 529 settled feasibility, the set is tiny (~114 rows corpus-wide); a straight build. Prod-verified on `119-hr-1` (the reconciliation bill's Senate vote-a-rama): 13 vote lines + 39 party-split fragments, the split summing to the tally in every sampled case (`119-hr-1`/`119-s-2`/`119-sconres-33`, e.g. a 50–50 VP-tiebreak reading R 50-3 party-line). **Two records for the ledger:** the **two amendment-row components are distinct** — the bill-hub row is an internal function inside `BillAmendments.tsx`; `components/AmendmentRow.tsx` is the *separate* `/amendments` corpus-feed row (HO 461) — so the HO 530 handoff's pathspec conflated them and the edit correctly landed in `BillAmendments.tsx` only (a SKILL note now, so future handoffs pathspec right); and **`votes.id` is a TEXT composite** (`senate-119-1-3`), not an integer. **No theme-% change — the combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92:** this ships the **Senate half** of the 519-named `recordedVotes` gap, but the **House linkage walk and the full per-member roll-call drill both stay banked**, so the gap is partially—not fully—discharged (a v1 cut on an existing pillar, the LDA/amendments precedent every prior surface held). **Banked, no fixed HO number** (they take the next free number when built — the HO 418 don't-pre-number lesson; the "531" the HO 530 handoff pinned to the House walk was premature since this sweep is 531): the **House amendment→vote linkage walk** (GO-WALK, build-ready, no further probe — a bounded `/actions` walk over HAMDTs-with-a-recorded-vote storing a persisted `amendment↔vote.id` link + a cron delta step), the **full per-member roll-call drill** (v1 ships the partisan *shape*; the who-voted-how list is the heavier v2 expand, `member_votes` has the data), and the **`/amendments` aggregate "voted" cut** (once both chambers' links exist). Docs: HO 531. **Also notes now run through HO 530.**

**Also (HO 532–533), amendment votes across both chambers + a latent-500 fix — the `recordedVotes` gap closed, no theme-% change.** The House tail HO 530/531 banked (GO-WALK, no probe — HO 529 §C confirmed 100% of `recordedVotes.rollNumber` resolve to existing `votes` rows), plus the cache-serialization bug the build surfaced. **HO 532 (`3c4c72c`), the House linkage walk.** The House has no cheap key (generic `On Agreeing to the Amendment` question, no number), so the amendment→vote link is recovered from each HAMDT's `/actions.recordedVotes` — a **linkage** backfill (`amendment_id ↔ vote_id`), NOT a member-vote one (positions already in the DB). A new **`amendment_votes`** link table (composite PK for the table+substantive list case), an **`amendment_vote_walked_at`** sentinel on `amendments` (sync-invisible — the amendments-sync `ON CONFLICT SET` names every column so it's never clobbered, the HO 459 pattern verified by reading the clause), and a partial `idx_amendments_hamdt` walk-queue index. `sync:amendment-votes --walk` (`lib/amendment-votes-walk.ts`) fetches each HAMDT's `/actions`, **constructs** the deterministic `house-{congress}-{session}-{rollNumber}` vote_id (House `votes.id` is composable — no lookup), upserts the link, stamps the sentinel (the queue-exit; the re-walk predicate keys on `update_date > walked_at`, so a HAMDT that *gains* a vote after its first walk is caught — the live-corpus edge the nominations immutable-committee case didn't have). A **bounded cron walk step** rides after the sync in `/api/cron/amendments` (240s/280s carve, cap 40/tick, non-fatal). The read is the **same `getBillAmendmentVotes`** (Step 1 widened to `IN ('SAMDT','HAMDT')`, a new Step 2b joins `amendment_votes → votes`; the party-split, tally, and render are chamber-agnostic and reused). Backfill: **267 HAMDT walked, 92 links / 90 amendments, 0 fetch errors** (`119-hamdt-5 → house-119-1-32`, Failed 182–226, 432 member_votes — the HO 529 §C case confirmed). **HO 533 (`85d6660`), two fixes, one blocking the other.** (1) **The latent prod 500 — the bigger find:** `getBillAmendmentVotes` returned a **`Map`**, but `unstable_cache` serializes its return and a Map round-trips to `{}` on a **cache HIT** — so `.get` was gone and `BillAmendments` threw `votes.get is not a function`, 500-ing **every amendment-bearing `/bill/[id]` for ~59 of every 60 minutes since HO 530 first shipped.** It hid because every "verified live" check landed in the cache-**miss** window right after each deploy (three deploys, three misses). Fixed by returning a plain **`Record<amendmentId, AmendmentVote[]>`** (serializes cleanly), consumer reads `votes[a.id]`. (2) **HO 533's own ask — the zero-split guard:** `VoteLine` now suppresses the D/R/I party-split fragment when it's entirely zero (the `member_votes`-lag case — the newest roll calls' member detail trails the vote-list sync, e.g. `house-119-2-269`), so those rows show the tally alone and self-heal on the next votes sync rather than printing a false `D 0-0 · R 0-0` (the "wrong is worse than absent" HO 448 disposition-dot principle). The Map fix was a **hard prerequisite** — the guard lives inside `VoteLine`, which never rendered while `BillAmendments` threw first, so both shipped in one commit. Prod-verified on the cache-**hit** path (3 consecutive hits): `119-hr-8800` 200 (was 500), 19 tallies / 18 splits (lagging row suppressed); `119-hr-1` (Senate) unchanged at 13; a repo-wide ~90-function audit confirmed `getBillAmendmentVotes` was the **only** Map/Set/Date-returning cached function. **No theme-% change — the combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92:** the House walk is the *linkage completion* of the same 519 `recordedVotes` gap (both chambers now render amendment votes), not a new surface, and the 500 fix is a correctness fix — recorded honestly as a regression that **shipped at HO 530 and lived latent for three deploys**, not a bar event. **Two process lessons (oddities):** never return a `Map`/`Set`/`Date`/class from an `unstable_cache` function (plain objects/arrays/primitives only — they round-trip to `{}`/`[]` on a hit, and tsc can't catch it); and **cache-miss verification right after a deploy does not exercise the cache-hit path** — hit it 2–3 times (or wait out the revalidate window) before calling a cached surface verified. **Banked, now both cleaner** (the House links unblocked them): the full per-member roll-call drill (both chambers ship the partisan *shape*; the who-voted-how list is the v2 expand) and the `/amendments` aggregate "voted" cut. Docs: HO 534. **Also notes now run through HO 533.**

**Also (HO 535), the participation *sort* on the /members roster — the ranked-list twin of the 527 dotplot, no theme-% change.** The banked participation sort/rank (the constraint the HO 523/525 carve-out was written for) — the "who misses the most votes" ranked list, complementing the HO 527 dotplot's distribution. Shipped `b565c13`. Adds a **MISSED** segment beside VOLUME / PASS RATE that orders the roster by each member's **119th missed-vote rate** (`SUM(not_voting)/COUNT(*)` over `member_votes`, `HAVING COUNT(*) >= PARTICIPATION_FLOOR=50` — the identical dotplot/B1 metric). **No new column — the shared `.mc-row` grid stays untouched:** `.mc-row` backs both `/members` AND `/lobbying`'s `FilingRow` (the filing rows fell back to the members grid), so widening it would reshape the lobbying rows; instead the existing **RATE column repurposes** to the missed-rate when the missed sort is active (amber vs passrate's green, `—` for null, the header swaps RATE→MISSED), leaving `.mc-row`/`.mc-r-num`/`/lobbying` byte-identical. **The delegate carve-out adapts HO 527 to a roster:** the dotplot *drops* the 6 non-voting delegates; a list can't drop members, so they're carved from the **ranking** (rate exposed NULL via the territorial predicate `chamber='house' AND state IN ('DC','AS','GU','MP','PR','VI')` → sort last + `—`, disclosed "non-voting delegates unranked") but kept in the list — same population-correctness principle, different mechanism. Threaded into **both** `getMembersRanked` (full list) and `getCommitteeRoster` (scoped view) via a shared `participationAggCte` + a shared `MISSED_CARVE_EXPR`, both gaining a **`votes` cache tag** (they read `member_votes` now, else they'd only refresh on the 86400s backstop — the `getChamberParticipationContext` precedent). **One spec bug the probe caught (oddities):** the handoff ordered by the bare `missed_pct` column, but SQLite resolves an unqualified column *inside an ORDER BY expression* against the input tables (`part_agg`'s raw rate), NOT the carved SELECT alias — so delegates (Radewagen 82%, Plaskett 50%) sorted to the top while displaying `—`; fixed by repeating the carve in the ORDER BY via the shared `MISSED_CARVE_EXPR`, re-probe confirmed. **No probe HO** (the missed-rate is the dotplot's own cheap ~536-row aggregate), but the build's throwaway probe verified the ranking: **TOP by MISSED = Wilson 29.0% → Hunt 24.6% → Kean 22.8%** (all voting members, matching the HO 527 dotplot tail), **BOTTOM = the 6 delegates + below-floor Graham showing `—`**. **No theme-% change — an enabling surface on `/members`; Member depth holds at 100** (the HO 425/527 dotplot precedent). **Banked:** a **career missed-rate sort** (its NULL-coverage set differs — the reason the dotplot's career toggle is banked) and the **career chamber-median context** on the hub (still the deliberate 4-stat-hero hold). Docs: HO 536. **Also notes now run through HO 535.**

**Also (HO 537), the `/amendments` "voted" cut — the last banked piece of the `recordedVotes` gap, no theme-% change.** HO 530/532/533 rendered amendment votes on the bill hub; the corpus surface still described the whole pillar through `latest_action_text` and couldn't say how many amendments actually drew a recorded floor vote. Shipped as four commits + a duplication fix, gated by a Step-0 cost/correctness probe. **STEP 0 (`amendments-voted-cost-537.ts`, `19a412e`):** the HO 461 inline cost-gate — 8 measurements. The equivalence gate (materializer set === live parse, per bill) PASSED; feed EXISTS EXPLAIN clean (composite-PK seek, no new index); containment clean (voted ⊆ acted, 0 with NULL action); tiebreak inert (0 same-date pairs). **Measurement 8 — a completeness detour that changed the design:** `votes` is append-only, so HO 529's "114 SAMDT" couldn't have fallen to the 97 the anchored matcher finds: of **161** senate votes that mention "Amdt", **97 are up-or-down** ("On the Amendment"), **64 are procedural** (`On the Motion to Table / Cloture Motion / Decision of the Chair S.Amdt. N`, + 50 budget-waiver `Amdt. No. N`). The pre-authorized "widen to capture the resolving residual" was **REJECTED on the data**: resolving an S.Amdt number is necessary, not sufficient — a procedural vote's yea/nay is on the MOTION, not the amendment (a `Motion to Table Failed` leaves the amendment ALIVE; `119-samdt-2310`/`4421` were WITHDRAWN, `119-samdt-2360` was ADOPTED 51–50 separately), and 3 of the 14 resolving residual already carry an anchored decisive vote a widen would double-line. **The anchored matcher is correct BY DESIGN** — it isolates the one class whose tally is renderable as support for the amendment. So: no widen, build as specced (oddities). **C1 (data, `92454f5`):** materialize the Senate links into `amendment_votes`, making it the single symmetric source of truth across chambers (House was HO 532's walk). Pure DB recompute (no API) — one `db.batch` delete-then-insert scoped by a `votes.chamber='senate'` join (not an id prefix), returning a real `changed` delta so the cron flushes `votes` only when the set moves (the recompute always rewrites, so row-count can't gate honestly). The Senate parser + LIKE moved to a **leaf `lib/amendment-vote-key.ts`** so the request-time parse and the sync-time materializer share ONE matcher (the `NEWS_ARTICLE_KEY_SQL`/`MISSED_CARVE_EXPR` anti-drift shape; leaf-safe so the `scripts/` materializer never drags `next/cache`); `getBillAmendmentVotes`' Senate path is byte-identical. A `--senate` CLI mode + a cron materialize step riding **before** the House walk (cheap-first, so a starved walk can't staleness the Senate links). **C2 (refactor, `acde5de`):** `VoteLine`/`voteVerb`/`dispositionColor` extracted to a shared `components/AmendmentVoteLine.tsx` (the corpus feed is the third consumer); byte-identical move verified by character-diff + `tsc` + build. **C3 (surface, `cb551c4`):** `getAmendmentsSummary` voted block (canonical/latest vote per amendment via `ROW_NUMBER` → `deriveDisposition`, the `list[0]` hub rule); `getAmendments` gains `voted` as a third disposition (EXISTS over resolved links) + page-scoped vote hydration (`hydratePageAmendmentVotes`, both chambers off the materialized table, party split FETCHED so the HO 533 zero-split guard reads true, plain `AmendmentVote[]` so `unstable_cache` round-trips clean); `AmendmentRow` renders the shared `VoteLine` with an authoritative-from-`votes.result` dot; the page gains a recorded-votes readout (authoritative agreed/failed), a `Voted` chip, a blurb chamber-contrast, and the exclusion-disclosure line. **Both cached fns gained the `votes` tag** — they read `votes`/`member_votes` now, so tags must follow the tables a query READS, not the pillar it belongs to (the HO 535 precedent; oddities). **Duplication fix (`c35837e`, own commit):** the C3 blurb repeated "187 drew a recorded floor vote" + "97 Senate · 90 House" verbatim with the readout; blurb now asserts qualitatively (claim), the readout owns the numbers (evidence) — the HO 509 double-print precedent. **Prod-verified on the cache-hit path (corpus at this snapshot 6,971 filed · 625 acted — HO 461's 6,788 was its own snapshot, left as written):** voted **187** (`42 agreed / 145 failed / 0 other`, `97 Senate / 90 House`); `?disposition=voted` 187 / `&type=HAMDT` 90 / `&type=SAMDT` 97; bare feed the full corpus (not 0/187 — the empty-`IN` guard holds); and the **bill-hub regression gate held exactly** — `119-hr-1` 13 lines, `119-hr-8800` 19 tallies / 18 splits — proving the leaf swap + `VoteLine` extraction didn't regress the hub. **Correction of the HO 529 reading (not a retro-edit of its block):** the "114 SAMDT" it recorded was procedural-inclusive; the genuine up-or-down count is **97** (oddities). **No theme-% change — a v1 cut on an existing pillar (the LDA/amendments precedent where 437 / 439–440 / 442 / 444 / 448 / 450 / 461 all held their bar); the combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92:** this ships the aggregate "voted" cut both 519 and 530/533 banked, discharging the `recordedVotes` gap's *aggregate* piece, but the **full per-member roll-call drill stays banked — this HO does NOT discharge it** (both chambers ship only the partisan shape; the who-voted-how list is the v2 expand) and a **procedural-outcome surface is newly banked** (needs a motion-aware model — invert tallies, distinguish cloture from adoption, suppress when an anchored vote exists). **Numbering — the 535 → 537 pointer jump skips no block:** HO 536 was the doc sweep *for* HO 535 (docs-only sweeps are owed no roadmap block — the HO 506 precedent), so a future reader should not hunt for a missing 536 block. Docs: HO 538. **Also notes now run through HO 537.**

**Also (HO 539–540), the `/vote/[id]` roll-call entity page + two residual cleanups — no theme-% change.** **HO 539 (residuals), briefly.** The `/amendments` blurb stopped asserting "near-evenly" (a hardcoded judgment on interpolated numbers — the HO 538 WATCH) and now states the contrast as **two percentages** — filings 96% Senate against recorded votes 52% Senate — guarded on `voted > 0`: self-updating prose with no adjective to rot, and no repeat of the readout's counts (which is why restoring the raw numbers wasn't the fix — it'd reopen the HO 509 double-print). Commit `588bed0`. Separately `df155de` wrote the **review-ref route** into SKILL and widened its scope beyond SKILL edits to **append-only roadmap blocks** (read-before-land text). **HO 540, the substance.** The banked per-member roll-call drill, built as a **page** not an expand-in-place: the fork was A (expand on `/bill/[id]`) vs B (a `/vote/[id]` entity page), B chosen. The argument is the HO 501 one — a roll call is an entity CBT already stores (`votes` at 1,513 rows with full positions in `member_votes`) whose data surfaced only as a fragment of other pages (a tally on the member hub, a split under an amendment). The corroborating find: **`getVotesByBill` existed, cached, with ZERO consumers** — the query for a bill's own passage votes had been written and never wired, the shape of a missing entity page, not a missing section. Three commits: **`41df8ed`** query layer (three NEW siblings — `getVoteById` reusing `VOTE_SELECT`/`rowToVote`, `getVoteMemberPositions`, `getVoteAmendmentContext` — never widening the shared SELECT), **`60d8c77`** the page + `VotePositionList` (flat sectioned, not `RecordTabs`), **`068dc7e`** inbound links (the tally → `/vote/[voteId]` on BOTH `/bill/[id]` and the `/amendments` feed via the shared `AmendmentVoteLine`; the member-hub roll number → `/vote/[id]`) + breadcrumb (`Legislation \ Votes`) + `pathToNavKey → "feed"`. **Step 0 (`vote-detail-cost-540.ts`):** House worst case is a **439-row list at 694ms cold** on the `member_votes` PK seek + the members PK join, so no index warranted; **4** votes carry a tally with zero positions; **890 of 1,513** votes carry `bill_id`, 623 don't, and **97** of those resolve via the `amendment_votes` link table. **Three decisions for the permanent record:** (1) **amendment context is fetched UNCONDITIONALLY, not as a null-`bill_id` fallback** — the Step-0 split showed a null-gate would show context on the 97 Senate amendment votes (no `bill_id`) and hide it on the 90 House ones (carry `bill_id`), the exact chamber asymmetry HO 537 spent its arc removing, reappearing in a different column (the HO 529 question-text asymmetry showing up again in `bill_id` presence). (2) **the party split is structural, not incidental** — `member_votes.bioguide_id` is deliberately not FK'd to `members`, so an unmatched row is expected; `getBillAmendmentVotes` INNER JOINs + skips unresolvable parties while the page LEFT JOINs, so the page applies the SAME skip-unless-D/R/I rule and the two agree regardless of join type (unmatched members still render — bioguide id, dim, unlinked, the `BillAmendments` sponsor idiom — because they did vote). (3) **two empty states, not one** — tally>0 with no positions is **lag** (4 votes, newest `house-119-2-269`, self-heals next votes sync); tally=0 with no positions is a **by-name/procedural** vote (`house-119-1-2`, Election of the Speaker) where nothing is pending and nothing ever arrives, and the zero tally is suppressed ("0–0" on a Speaker election is meaningless). **No theme-% change — the combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92:** this discharges the per-member roll-call drill banked at HO 530/533, but it's a v1 entity surface (the LDA/amendments precedent where 437 / 439–440 / 442 / 444 / 448 / 450 / 461 / 537 all held). **`/vote/[id]` is CROSS-CUTTING** — it serves bills, amendments, nominations AND the member hub — so it doesn't sit cleanly under any one bar; whether roll calls warrant their own line is for the next full re-derivation, not this sweep. Docs: HO 541. **Also notes now run through HO 540.**

**Also (HO 542), the `/bill/[id]` VOTES tab — the bill's own passage/procedural roll calls, no theme-% change.** The HO 541 sweep flagged `getVotesByBill` as **written, cached, and UNCONSUMED** — `/bill/[id]` rendered its *amendments'* votes (HO 530/532/533) but never the bill's *own* passage / motion-to-recommit / cloture / motion-to-commit roll calls, because the query for them had been written and never wired (the same orphan HO 540 read as the shape of a missing entity page). HO 542 wires it into the HO 510 `RecordTabs` box as a VOTES tab, each row → `/vote/[id]`. **STEP 0 (`bill-votes-overlap-542.ts`, `630833c`):** the one real unknown was overlap with the Amendments tab — `getVotesByBill` returns *every* vote carrying the bill's `bill_id`, and House amendment votes carry `bill_id` (HO 532), so some of its rows are already shown under Amendments. Read-only measurement: **890** bill_id-carrying votes across **467** bills; **92** are amendment votes (**all 92 House-linked via `amendment_votes`; 0 Senate-pattern**) → **798 own votes across all 467 bills** (p50 **1** · p90 **2** · max **24**), overlap concentrated exactly on amendment-heavy bills (`119-hr-8800`: 19 of 21; `119-hr-1`: 0 of 24). **The finding that shaped the build:** the exclusion set is **House-link only** — Senate amendment votes are question-matched and **don't carry `bill_id`** (the HO 540 bill_id-asymmetry, the HO 529 question-text asymmetry once more), so they never enter `getVotesByBill` and the belt-and-suspenders Senate-pattern check the handoff pre-authorized is a provable no-op (0 leak). So the exclusion is a plain `Set<string>` over the already-fetched `amendmentVotes` map values — no `lib/amendment-vote-key.ts` import in the page (oddities). **The build (`61cacd2`):** `getVotesByBill(bill.id)` added to the page `Promise.all`; `ownVotes = billVotes.filter(v => !amendmentVoteIds.has(v.id))`; a NEW `components/BillVoteRow.tsx` (server summary row — tally · date · question+result · `#{rollCall}` → `/vote/[id]`; **NOT** `MemberVoteRow` [member-scoped chip] nor `VotePositionList` [the heavy per-member breakdown] — the bill hub has no per-member context; the "new sibling, don't widen" discipline), with the **HO 540 tally-suppression** on an all-zero roll call (a by-name/procedural vote where "0–0" is meaningless). The tab inserted **between amendments and lobbying** (passage = the culminating floor action on the whole bill; amendments act on parts, lobbying is orthogonal-last), **omit-at-zero** like every other bill-page tab, `initialKey` untouched (own-vote counts are small, so Votes never steals the initial tab on a heavy bill; a passage-only bill opens on Votes). CSS **additive under `.bvote-*`** — no `.vote-row`/`.rec-*`/`.mc-*`/`.bxp-*`/`.bdp-*` edit; one deliberate deviation from `MemberVoteRow` — the roll# stays visible on mobile because here it's the *sole* link to the vote page. **Discharges the backlog QUEUED "bill-hub passage-votes section" (HO 418)** — see DONE. **No theme-% change — a v1 cut wiring an existing orphaned query onto an existing pillar (the LDA/amendments precedent where 437 / 439–440 / 442 / 444 / 448 / 450 / 461 / 537 / 540 all held); the combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92.** **Prod-verified on the cache-hit path:** `119-hr-8800` Votes **2** (passage + motion to recommit) with Amendments still **19** (21 unique `/vote/` links = 2 own + 19 amendment — no double-count, Amendments byte-identical); `119-hr-5371` Votes **21**; `119-hr-3034` (introduced-never-voted) **no VOTES tab**. **Owed eyeball (WATCH):** the tally-suppression (`bvote-none`) path is tsc-verified but **unexercised** — none of the three samples had a by-name own-vote — render-confirm on the next one (the HO 540 empty-state WATCH posture). Docs: HO 542 (this sweep — backlog DONE + WATCH, oddities, the SKILL `getVotesByBill` reconciliation). **Also notes now run through HO 542.**

**Also (HO 543–544), the `/lobbying` corpus fbar's sort segment (RECENT ⇄ VOLUME) + bill-linked toggle — the first of the HO 486 fbar cuts, no theme-% change.** The HO 486 chrome restructure deferred the fbar's real filters (each needs a query, not markup); HO 544 ships two of the three (sort + bill-linked), gated by a Step-0 cost/shape probe. **HO 543 (`aabc8a8`, read-only probe).** Priced the sort × filter over the corpus feed and read GO — but on the **cheapest component**, which is exactly the trap the build then walked into (oddities). **HO 544 (`e640a0d`).** `getRecentFilings` gains `sort` (`"recent"` default / `"volume"` = per-filing activity count, "meatiest filings") × `billLinked`. **The default path (recent + unfiltered) is the byte-identical pre-544 SQL** — same `idx_lda_filings_dt_posted` ordered walk, so the untouched common case can't regress. **The mid-build catch (the load-bearing find):** the 543 probe timed VOLUME as a bare `GROUP BY filing_uuid` over `lda_activities` (index-only, **340–440ms**) — but the shipped feed has to join back to `lda_filings` to hydrate the row, and the naive shape (LEFT JOIN from `lda_filings`, order by the joined count) planned an **AUTOMATIC COVERING INDEX on the materialized agg → ~18s cold** (a build-time EXPLAIN caught it). The fix **drives from the agg (INNER JOIN)**, PK-seeking into `lda_filings` — the ~295 zero-activity filings the INNER JOIN drops have `activity_count 0`, sort dead-last past the 40-page clamp, so the visible feed is identical to a LEFT JOIN (a `do NOT "fix" this to a LEFT JOIN` comment guards it). The `dt_posted` hint is **dropped** on VOLUME (order is by count, not date) and **kept** on recent+linked. **The required addition on review — the HO 440/448 guard:** the read + hydrate are wrapped **try/catch → `{items:[], page, pageSize}`**. VOLUME's ~7s worst cold miss sits close to the 10s `DB_REQUEST_TIMEOUT_MS` wall (the 543 probe saw remote Turso vary up to ~5×), and this is the ONE live LDA read on `/lobbying` — an abort would 500 the WHOLE page (rollup/rail/crosswalk/firms), not just the feed, so it degrades to an empty feed instead (the same guard every LDA surface has carried since 440; commented so it isn't stripped as defensive noise). Controls are **unscoped-feed-only** (carried across pagination + expand) and **hidden when scoped** (`?issue=` serves the precomputed `drill.recent` sample — the HO 437 >25s per-issue wall — which can't be re-sorted live). **Prod-verified on the cache-hit path (2× per URL):** all 4 combos 200 (VOLUME cold **7.08s** → warm 1.83s — right at the WATCH figure; volume+linked 2.84s → 0.36s); unscoped shows the VOLUME control, `?issue=TAX` hides it; `?page=999` clamps the feed byte-identical to page 40 (only the echoed `?page=` in the expand `toggleHref` differs). **No theme-% change — a v1 filter cut on the existing LDA pillar (437/439–440/442/444/486 precedent); the combined `Lobbying / amendments / nominations` bar holds at 92.** **Two records for the ledger (docs, HO 544 this sweep):** the **oddities lesson** — *a probe that measures an aggregate in isolation has not measured the feed query that joins back to hydrate rows; cost-probe the shape you'll actually ship, joins included, not its cheapest component* (a sibling of the HO 340/437 "clean EXPLAIN says nothing about cold latency" entry — cross-referenced); and the **WATCH** — VOLUME cold-miss headroom (~7s vs the 10s wall, corpus grew 108,707 → 112,411 this arc, sort×linked took the cache-key surface ~40 → ~160 keys so cold misses hit ~4× more often), **promote to a fix if it ever aborts** (the fix is a precomputed `activity_count` column on `lda_filings` — the 543 probe's noted optional upgrade, turning VOLUME into a ~45ms indexed walk, and the natural home if search wants the same). **The third fbar cut — registrant/client SEARCH — stays QUEUED as its own FTS follow-on** (a `LIKE` over 112k rows full-scans; wants an `lda_filings` FTS5 index, the `bills_fts` shape).
